	{

	var gGreenlightAcceptedRedirectDialog = null;

	function RequestSketchfabDetails( previewID, modelID )
	{
		var elem = $("thumb_sketchfab_model_" + previewID );
		var img = elem.down( '.thumb_sketchfab_img' );
		$J.get( "https://sketchfab.com/oembed?url=https://sketchfab.com/models/" + modelID )
		.done(
			function( json ) {
				img.src = json.thumbnail_url;
			}
		)
	}

	Event.observe(document, 'dom:loaded', function() {
		if( searchText )
			HighlightSearchText( searchText, $('highlightContent') );

			gGreenlightAcceptedRedirectDialog.SetRemoveContentOnDismissal( false );

			});

	function CloseGreenlightAcceptedRedirectDialog()
	{
		gGreenlightAcceptedRedirectDialog.Dismiss();
		gGreenlightAcceptedRedirectDialog = null;
	}

		<div class="parentCollections">
								<div data-panel="{&quot;focusable&quot;:true,&quot;clickOnActivate&quot;:true}" role="button" class="parentCollection" onClick="top.location.href='https://steamcommunity.com/workshop/filedetails/?id=3734620490'">
						 <div class="parentCollectionTitle">The M-13 Family B42 Project Zomboid Multiplayer Server Modlist</div>
												<div class="parentCollectionRating"><img class="fileRating" src="https://community.fastly.steamstatic.com/public/images/sharedfiles/not-yet.png?v=2"></div>
												<div class="parentCollectionNumChildren">120 Inhalte</div>

			<div class="detailBox altFooter">
				<div class="workshopItemDescriptionTitle">Beschreibung</div>
								<div class="workshopItemDescription" id="highlightContent"><div class="bb_h1">Mod confirmed working with:</div><b>* Build 41.78.20<br>* Build 42.21.0<br>* Multiplayer Compatible</b><br><br><div class="bb_h1">Mod Information:</div><b>Workshop ID: 2776874515<br>Mod ID: M13ReadingTweaks</b><br><br><div class="bb_h1">Mod Description:</div><br><b><u>What does this mod do?</u></b><br>Simply put this mod allows you to read faster (or slower) and allows you to even read a book while you walk.<br><img src="https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/11912088753111341539/075947D86BE59E72AADDFB6250F343CC6BF70D68/?imw=5000&amp;imh=5000&amp;ima=fit&amp;impolicy=Letterbox&amp;imcolor=%23000000&amp;letterbox=false" /><br>The mod also has it's own Sandbox options you can use to configure if you want to be able to read while walking, how fast or slow you read AND if you want to read faster when sitting down!<br><br><b><u>How does it work?</u></b><br>Either start a new game and adjust the settings under <b>[Custom Sandbox]</b><br>OR install a mod that allows you to <a class="bb_link" href="https://steamcommunity.com/workshop/browse/?appid=108600&amp;searchtext=change+sandbox+options" target="_blank" rel="" ><b>[Change Sandbox Options]</b></a> for a game you've already started.<br><br>You can find all the settings under <b>[M-13's Reading Tweaks]</b><br><img src="https://i.imgur.com/EmcyVXB.jpeg" /><br><br><b>Minutes per page (Build 41 only)</b><br>This value determines how fast or slow you read books while standing or walking.<br>The default for SOLO is 2.0, The default for Servers is 1.0<br>The lower this value, the faster you read.<br>(For example: setting this to 0.1 will make you read really fast)<br><b>B42 Users can find this setting under &quot;Character&quot;, it's called &quot;Minutes Per Skill Book Page&quot;</b><br><br><b>Blur while Reading (Build 42.15+ Only)</b><br>This toggle enables a blur around the player when they are reading.<br><br><b>Blur Darkness Intensity (Build 42.15+ Only)</b> <br>This value allows you to set how Dark the blur around the player is, 0 will practically disable the blur, while 100 is pitch black.<br><br><b>Sitting Multiplier</b><br>This value multiplies the reading speed, but only applies to when you are sitting.<br>The Higher this value, the faster you read.<br>Pre 42.15 this only applied to ground and furniture, from 42.15 onwards, this applies to sitting in Vehicles as well.<br><br><b>Read while walking</b><br>Enable this to read a book while walking.<br><br><b><u>Does this mod work with other mods?</u></b><br>Yes, UNLESS the other mod(s) mess with ISReadABook.lua, then stuff might break<br>(Check Incompatible Mods discussion for known mods)<br><br><b><u>Does this mod work with Multiplayer?</u></b><br>B41: Yes, B42 42.15+.<br><br><b><u><div class="bb_h1">After an update my character is reading slower/faster then they used to, why?</div></u></b><br>Sometimes i am forced to change stuff in the mod that forces the game to reset certain values back to default<br>The first good example of this was when B42 introduced <b>Minutes per Skill Book Page</b> as a vanilla sandbox setting, which made this mod's <b>Minutes per page</b> setting absolete.<br>There is nothing i can do about this but you won't have to start a new game because the fix is pretty simple:<br><br><blockquote class="bb_blockquote">Use <b>DEBUG</b> mode (or a mod) to change your savegame's sandbox settings.<br>(Starting a new game also works, but that would be overkill right?)</blockquote><br>How do i enable debug mode?<br><blockquote class="bb_blockquote">To enable debug mode:<br>- Rightclick on Project Zomboid in your Steam Library<br>- Click on Properties<br>- In the box under <b>Launch Options</b> type:  <b>-debug</b><br><br>- Launch Project Zomboid and load your save.<br>- Click on the WASP looking icon on the left side of the screen, this is the <b>DEBUG MENU</b>.<br>- Click on <b>Sandbox Settings</b><br><br>If you want ALL reading to go faster:<br>- Click on the <b>Character</b> tab and look for <b>Minutes per skill book page</b><br>- Lower it's value to make reading go faster.<br><br>If you only want reading to go faster when sitting:<br>- Click on the <b>M-13's Reading Tweaks</b> tab and look for <b>Sitting Multiplier</b><br>- The higher this number, the faster you read when sitting on the ground or on furniture.<br><br>After you are done changing values click <b>Apply Changes</b><br>If you want to disable DEBUG MODE, close your game and remove -debug from the launch options.<br><b>The settings you changed will only take effect AFTER you reloaded your save game.</b></blockquote><br><b><u>What if i do not want to use this mod anymore?</u></b><br>This mod can be removed from your game without consequences.<br><br><b><u>Does this work for Multiplayer?</u></b><br>For B41: Yes it does, but it overwrites the &quot;MinutesPerPage&quot; value in your server's sandbox settings.<br>When Multiplayer for B42 releases, i expect no issues regarding this either.<br><br>It <i>should</i> also work in Co-op, but i have not tested this.<br><br><div class="bb_h1">Translations:</div><ul class="bb_ul"><li> English<br></li><li> Dutch<br></li><li> Traditional Chinese (By: <a class="bb_link" href="https://steamcommunity.com/profiles/76561198275588498" target="_blank" rel="" >Nemo</a>)</li></ul><br><b>Like my content?<br><img src="https://i.imgur.com/yOjbYWq.gif" /><br>And maybe consider buying me a cup of coffee</b><br><a class="bb_link" href="https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fko-fi.com%2Fgeneralm" target="_blank" rel=" noopener" ><img src="https://steamuserimages-a.akamaihd.net/ugc/932676970227717956/B46AF8E8B0CA785280C03000DAB1BD182C1DDD51/" /></a><span class="bb_link_host">[ko-fi.com]</span><br><br><div class="bb_h1">Mod Permissions:</div><blockquote class="bb_blockquote"><b>This mod's permissions fall under TIS's policy <a class="bb_link" href="https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Ftheindiestone.com%2Fforums%2Findex.php%3F%2Ftopic%2F2530-mod-permissions%2F" target="_blank" rel=" noopener" >On Lockdown</a><span class="bb_link_host">[theindiestone.com]</span><br>This mod cannot be modified by anyone else, for anything else, at all, ever. Even if the user disappears, he or she would much rather you didn't touch the fruits of their hard labour.</b></blockquote><a class="bb_link" href="https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=http%3A%2F%2Ftheindiestone.com%2Fforums%2Findex.php%2Ftopic%2F2530-mod-permissions%2F%3Fp%3D36479" target="_blank" rel=" noopener" ><img src="https://imgur.com/R6mrPj8.png" /></a><span class="bb_link_host">[theindiestone.com]</span><br><br><div class="bb_h1">Please check the <b><u><a class="bb_link" href="https://steamcommunity.com/sharedfiles/filedetails/changelog/2776874515" target="_blank" rel="" >change notes</a></u></b> before posting, thank you!</div><br><div class="bb_h1">My Other Mods:</div><a class="bb_link" href="https://steamcommunity.com/sharedfiles/filedetails/?id=2930717154" target="_blank" rel="" ><img src="https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2010330396162880397/A431639D246F7412B048A5AF238C457B5BC2B21A/?imw=268&amp;imh=268&amp;ima=fit&amp;impolicy=Letterbox&amp;imcolor=%23000000&amp;letterbox=true" /></a></div>
